Move:     +58bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The C10 real-yield leg fired: the 10-year TIPS real yield hit 2.47pp on 2026-07-31, up 0.58pp from its trailing-90 low of 1.89pp, crossing the 2x-trigger threshold that escalates it to HIGH.

内容:
C10 的实际收益率分项触发：10 年期 TIPS 实际收益率在 2026-07-31 达到 2.47pp，较过去 90 日低点 1.89pp 上升 0.58pp，触及 2 倍阈值，升级为 HIGH。

Why it matters:
A sharp real-yield spike tightens financial conditions and raises the discount rate applied to long-duration AI-capex cash flows, directly pressuring the valuations that assume years of payoff before hardware depreciates out. It's a liquidity-side stress tell independent of any single company's fundamentals.

影响:
实际收益率急升会收紧金融条件，抬高对长久期 AI capex 现金流所用的贴现率，直接压制那些依赖多年后才能回本的估值假设。这是独立于单一公司基本面的流动性端压力信号。

What this means:
APLD triggered a covenant default, per the C2 distress screen.

内容:
根据 C2 困境筛查,APLD 触发了契约违约(covenant default)。

Why it matters:
Covenant default is a hard-data credit-stress signal, not sentiment — it means the neocloud's lenders now have contractual leverage (accelerated repayment, forced asset sales) at a moment when the whole sector's economics depend on cheap, continuous refinancing of AI-datacenter buildouts. One neocloud default raises the read-through risk for the rest of the leveraged buildout cohort.

影响:
契约违约是硬数据级别的信用压力信号,不是情绪指标——这意味着贷款方现在拥有合同赋予的强制手段(提前还款、强制资产处置),而整个 neocloud 板块的经济模型恰恰依赖于持续、廉价的再融资来支撑 AI 数据中心的建设。一家 neocloud 违约,会提高其他高杠杆建设同行的连带风险。

<a href="https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMiZkFVX3lxTE5wSTVEc1c2M3FpT19URU9zZnpVRFdIUjd2eVliUTBvd0ZNdGQySndDZlZGaGVjXzdZaGpLNUZZQkFxcU5MV1BUcW5IWlVXbEFHQjNRbFZCcjBXcHFWbm9MbVA4bldXZw?oc=5" target="_blank">SSD Prices Double as NAND Shortage Hits Gaming PCs [2026]</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<font color="#6f6f6f">tech-insider.org</font>

What this means:
A Google News RSS item reports SSD prices doubling as a NAND shortage hits gaming PCs, per tech-insider.org.

内容:
一则 Google News RSS 消息称，受 NAND 短缺影响，SSD 价格翻倍，游戏 PC 首当其冲，来源为 tech-insider.org。

Why it matters:
NAND/DRAM shortages ripple into HBM and enterprise memory pricing, which raises AI accelerator BOM costs and squeezes hyperscaler capex efficiency — a supply-side stress tell for the AI-infra buildout, though this single consumer-SSD item is unconfirmed and needs corroboration before being treated as systemic.

影响:
NAND/DRAM 短缺往往会传导至 HBM 及企业级内存价格，从而推高 AI 加速卡的物料成本，压缩超大规模厂商的资本开支效率——这是 AI 基础设施建设的供给侧压力信号，但目前仅为单条消费级 SSD 报道，尚未获交叉验证，不宜直接视为系统性问题。

内容:
